# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in <Operations> with a specfication.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}
class Airport {
    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>

}

    CONFIRMED

# Operation Specifications
Customer::confirm
Summary: Confirms an existing reservation belonging to this customer, provided the flight has not departed and is still open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. Search through all bookings of this customer. For each Booking b:
       For each Reservation r in b.reservations:
           If r.id equals reservationID:
               a. Let f = r.flight.
               b. Check if now >= f.departureTime. If true, return false (flight already departed).
               c. Check if f.openForBooking is false. If false, return false (flight closed).
               d. Set r.status = CONFIRMED.
               e. Return true.
  2. If reservationID not found in any of this customer's bookings, return false.
Related Features: bookings list, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Reservation.flight, Flight.departureTime, Flight.openForBooking
Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, EDate, ReservationStatus
Input: reservationID : String — the unique ID of the reservation to confirm.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp.
Output: boolean — true if confirmation was successful; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: reservationID is not null.
Post-condition: If true, the reservation's status is CONFIRMED.

Customer::cancel
Summary: Cancels an existing reservation belonging to this customer, provided the flight has not departed and is still open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. Search through all bookings of this customer. For each Booking b:
       For each Reservation r in b.reservations:
           If r.id equals reservationID:
               a. Let f = r.flight.
               b. Check if now >= f.departureTime. If true, return false (flight already departed).
               c. Check if f.openForBooking is false. If false, return false (flight closed).
               d. Set r.status = CANCELED.
               e. Return true.
  2. If reservationID not found in any of this customer's bookings, return false.
Related Features: bookings list, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Reservation.flight, Flight.departureTime, Flight.openForBooking
Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, EDate, ReservationStatus
Input: reservationID : String — the unique ID of the reservation to cancel.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp.
Output: boolean — true if cancellation was successful; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: reservationID is not null.
Post-condition: If true, the reservation's status is CANCELED.

Customer::addBooking
Summary: Creates a new booking for this customer on a given flight for a list of passengers, after validating no duplicate passengers and that the flight has not departed.
Algorithm:
  1. Check if f is null or f.openForBooking is false. If so, return false.
  2. Check if now >= f.departureTime. If true, return false (flight already departed or departing now).
  3. For each passenger name in listOfPassengerNames:
       a. Check if any existing Reservation in f.reservations has a passenger with the same name. If found, return false (duplicate).
  4. Create a new Booking object:
       - customer = this
       - reservations = new empty list
  5. For each passenger name in listOfPassengerNames:
       a. Call this new Booking's createReservation(f, passengerName, now).
  6. Add this new Booking to this customer's bookings list.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: bookings list, Flight.openForBooking, Flight.departureTime, Flight.reservations, Reservation.passenger.name
Related Classes: Flight, Booking, Reservation, Passenger, EDate
Input: f : Flight — the flight to book.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp.
       listOfPassengerNames : List<String> — list of passenger names (non-null, non-empty).
Output: boolean — true if booking was successful; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: f is not null, listOfPassengerNames is not null and contains at least one name.
Post-condition: If true, a new Booking is added to this customer, containing a PENDING Reservation for each passenger, and those reservations are also added to the flight.

The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}
class Airport {
    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}

    name : String
}

    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}

    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml

# Operation Specifications
Customer::confirm
Summary: Confirms an existing reservation belonging to this customer, provided the flight has not departed and is still open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. Search through all bookings of this customer. For each Booking b:
       For each Reservation r in b.reservations:
           If r.id equals reservationID:
               a. Let f = r.flight.
               b. Check if now >= f.departureTime. If true, return false (flight already departed).
               c. Check if f.openForBooking is false. If false, return false (flight closed).
               d. Set r.status = CONFIRMED.
               e. Return true.
  2. If reservationID not found in any of this customer's bookings, return false.
Related Features: bookings list, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Reservation.flight, Flight.departureTime, Flight.openForBooking
Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, EDate, ReservationStatus
Input: reservationID : String — the unique ID of the reservation to confirm.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp.
Output: boolean — true if confirmation was successful; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: reservationID is not null.
Post-condition: If true, the reservation's status is CONFIRMED.

Customer::cancel
Summary: Cancels an existing reservation belonging to this customer, provided the flight has not departed and is still open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. Search through all bookings of this customer. For each Booking b:
       For each Reservation r in b.reservations:
           If r.id equals reservationID:
               a. Let f = r.flight.
               b. Check if now >= f.departureTime. If true, return false (flight already departed).
               c. Check if f.openForBooking is false. If false, return false (flight closed).
               d. Set r.status = CANCELED.
               e. Return true.
  2. If reservationID not found in any of this customer's bookings, return false.
Related Features: bookings list, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Reservation.flight, Flight.departureTime, Flight.openForBooking
Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, EDate, ReservationStatus
Input: reservationID : String — the unique ID of the reservation to cancel.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp.
Output: boolean — true if cancellation was successful; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: reservationID is not null.
Post-condition: If true, the reservation's status is CANCELED.

Customer::addBooking
Summary: Creates a new booking for this customer on a given flight for a list of passengers, after validating no duplicate passengers and that the flight has not departed.
Algorithm:
  1. Check if f is null or f.openForBooking is false. If so, return false.
  2. Check if now >= f.departureTime. If true, return false (flight already departed or departing now).
  3. For each passenger name in listOfPassengerNames:
       a. Check if any existing Reservation in f.reservations has a passenger with the same name. If found, return false (duplicate).
  4. Create a new Booking object:
       - customer = this
       - reservations = new empty list
  5. For each passenger name in listOfPassengerNames:
       a. Call this new Booking's createReservation(f, passengerName, now).
  6. Add this new Booking to this customer's bookings list.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: bookings list, Flight.openForBooking, Flight.departureTime, Flight.reservations, Reservation.passenger.name
Related Classes: Flight, Booking, Reservation, Passenger, EDate
Input: f : Flight — the flight to book.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp.
       listOfPassengerNames : List<String> — list of passenger names (non-null, non-empty).
Output: boolean — true if booking was successful; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: f is not null, listOfPassengerNames is not null and contains at least one name.
Post-condition: If true, a new Booking is added to this customer, containing a PENDING Reservation for each passenger, and those reservations are also added to the flight.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

Related Classes: City
Input: c : City — the city to add.
Output: void
Pre-condition: c is not null.
Post-condition: servesForCities contains c.

    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Flight::removeStopover
Summary: Removes a stopover from the flight, ensuring the current time is before the stopover's arrival time.
Algorithm:
  1. If stop is null, return false.
  2. Check if now < stop.arrivalTime. If not, return false (stopover already in progress or past).
  3. Find stop in this.stopovers list. If not found, return false.
  4. Remove stop from this.stopovers list.
  5. Return true.
Related Features: stopovers list, Stopover.arrivalTime
Related Classes: Stopover, EDate
Input: stop : Stopover — the stopover to remove.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp.
Output: boolean — true if the stopover was removed successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: stop is not null.
Post-condition: If true, stop is removed from the flight's stopovers list.

Flight::getConfirmedReservations
Summary: Retrieves all confirmed reservations for this flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.openForBooking is false, return an empty list.
  2. Create an empty result list: List<Reservation> result.
  3. For each Reservation r in this.reservations:
       If r.status == CONFIRMED, add r to result.
  4. Return result.
Related Features: openForBooking, reservations list, Reservation.status
Related Classes: Reservation, ReservationStatus
Input: None
Output: List<Reservation> — list of confirmed reservations; empty list if flight is closed or none exist.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns a list (possibly empty) of confirmed reservations.

Flight::addStopover
Summary: Adds a stopover to the flight, ensuring the stopover's times are temporally consistent with the flight's schedule and current time.
Algorithm:
  1. If stop is null, return false.
  2. Validate that stop.arrivalTime and stop.departureTime are in "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss" format. If invalid, return false.
  3. Check: now < stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime. If not, return false.
  4. Check that stop.airport is not null and that stop.airport != this.departureAirport and stop.airport != this.arrivalAirport (optional business rule, but not explicitly required; we'll allow any airport).
  5. Add stop to this.stopovers list.
  6. Return true.
Related Features: stopovers list, Flight.departureTime, Flight.arrivalTime
Related Classes: Stopover, Airport, EDate
Input: stop : Stopover — the stopover to add.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp.
Output: boolean — true if the stopover was added successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: stop is not null.
Post-condition: If true, stop is added to the flight's stopovers list.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Operation Specifications
Airline::addFlight
Summary: Adds a new Flight object to the airline's list of flights.
Algorithm:
  1. Check if the flight is null. If null, return.
  2. Check if the flight's ID already exists in the list (flights). If yes, return (no duplicate).
  3. Append the flight to the flights list.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f : Flight — the flight object to be added. Must not be null and must have a unique ID.
Output: void
Pre-condition: f is not null. f.id is unique among all flights in the airline.
Post-condition: The flights list contains the new flight f.

Airline::closeFlight
Summary: Closes an open flight that has not yet departed, but only if the current date is not the same calendar day as the flight's departure. If successful, it cancels all confirmed reservations.
Algorithm:
  1. Find the Flight object with the given flightId in the flights list. If not found, return false.
  2. Check if flight.openForBooking is true. If false, return false.
  3. Check if flight.departureTime is after now (flight has not departed). If not, return false.
  4. Check if the calendar date of now (yyyy-MM-dd) equals the calendar date of flight.departureTime. If equal, return false (closing not allowed on same calendar day).
  5. Set flight.openForBooking = false.
  6. For each Reservation r in flight.reservations:
       - If r.status == CONFIRMED, set r.status = CANCELED.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: flights list, Flight.openForBooking, Flight.departureTime, Flight.reservations list, Reservation.status
Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input: flightId : String — the unique ID of the flight to close.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp (e.g., "2024-12-08 13:00:00").
Output: boolean — true if the flight was successfully closed; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: flightId must correspond to an existing flight in the airline.
Post-condition: If true, the flight's openForBooking is false, and all previously confirmed reservations on that flight are now CANCELED.

Airline::searchFlights
Summary: Searches for flights that match a given origin airport ID, destination airport ID, and departure date.
Algorithm:
  1. Create an empty result list: List<Flight> result.
  2. For each Flight f in flights:
       a. If f.departureAirport.id equals origin AND f.arrivalAirport.id equals dest AND the calendar date of f.departureTime equals the calendar date of date, then add f to result.
  3. Return result.
Related Features: flights list, Flight.departureAirport, Flight.arrivalAirport, Flight.departureTime
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
Input: origin : String — the ID of the departure airport.
       dest : String — the ID of the arrival airport.
       date : EDate — the departure date to filter by (only the date part matters).
Output: List<Flight> — list of matching flights; empty list if none found.
Pre-condition: origin and dest are non-null strings.
Post-condition: Returns a list (possibly empty) of flights matching the search criteria.

Airline::removeFlight
Summary: Removes a Flight object from the airline's list of flights.
Algorithm:
  1. Check if f is null. If null, return.
  2. Find f in the flights list. If found, remove it.
  3. If not found, do nothing.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f : Flight — the flight object to be removed.
Output: void
Pre-condition: f is not null.
Post-condition: The flights list no longer contains f.

Airline::publishFlight
Summary: Publishes a flight to make it available for booking, after validating timestamps, temporal consistency, and route integrity.
Algorithm:
  1. Check if f is null. If null, return false.
  2. Check if f.openForBooking is false. If true, return false (already published).
  3. Validate that f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are in "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss" format. If invalid, return false.
  4. Check: now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime. If not, return false.
  5. Check: f.departureAirport != f.arrivalAirport. If equal, return false.
  6. Set f.openForBooking = true.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: flights list, Flight.openForBooking, Flight.departureTime, Flight.arrivalTime, Flight.departureAirport, Flight.arrivalAirport
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
Input: f : Flight — the flight to publish.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp.
Output: boolean — true if the flight was successfully published; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: f is not null and is not already published (openForBooking == false).
Post-condition: If true, f.openForBooking is set to true.

 * revised description for operation_1
 */

class_1::operation_2
/*
 * revised description for this operation_2
 */

It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}

    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}

    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}

    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}

    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport

    stopovers : List<Stopover>

    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean

    name : String
}

    flight : Flight

    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED

Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml

# Operation Specifications
Airline::addFlight
Summary: Adds a new Flight object to the airline's list of flights.
Algorithm:
  1. Check if the flight is null. If null, return.
  2. Check if the flight's ID already exists in the list (flights). If yes, return (no duplicate).
  3. Append the flight to the flights list.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f : Flight — the flight object to be added. Must not be null and must have a unique ID.
Output: void
Pre-condition: f is not null. f.id is unique among all flights in the airline.
Post-condition: The flights list contains the new flight f.

Airline::closeFlight
Summary: Closes an open flight that has not yet departed, but only if the current date is not the same calendar day as the flight's departure. If successful, it cancels all confirmed reservations.
Algorithm:
  1. Find the Flight object with the given flightId in the flights list. If not found, return false.
  2. Check if flight.openForBooking is true. If false, return false.
  3. Check if flight.departureTime is after now (flight has not departed). If not, return false.
  4. Check if the calendar date of now (yyyy-MM-dd) equals the calendar date of flight.departureTime. If equal, return false (closing not allowed on same calendar day).
  5. Set flight.openForBooking = false.
  6. For each Reservation r in flight.reservations:
       - If r.status == CONFIRMED, set r.status = CANCELED.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: flights list, Flight.openForBooking, Flight.departureTime, Flight.reservations list, Reservation.status
Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input: flightId : String — the unique ID of the flight to close.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp (e.g., "2024-12-08 13:00:00").
Output: boolean — true if the flight was successfully closed; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: flightId must correspond to an existing flight in the airline.
Post-condition: If true, the flight's openForBooking is false, and all previously confirmed reservations on that flight are now CANCELED.

Airline::searchFlights
Summary: Searches for flights that match a given origin airport ID, destination airport ID, and departure date.
Algorithm:
  1. Create an empty result list: List<Flight> result.
  2. For each Flight f in flights:
       a. If f.departureAirport.id equals origin AND f.arrivalAirport.id equals dest AND the calendar date of f.departureTime equals the calendar date of date, then add f to result.
  3. Return result.
Related Features: flights list, Flight.departureAirport, Flight.arrivalAirport, Flight.departureTime
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
Input: origin : String — the ID of the departure airport.
       dest : String — the ID of the arrival airport.
       date : EDate — the departure date to filter by (only the date part matters).
Output: List<Flight> — list of matching flights; empty list if none found.
Pre-condition: origin and dest are non-null strings.
Post-condition: Returns a list (possibly empty) of flights matching the search criteria.

Airline::removeFlight
Summary: Removes a Flight object from the airline's list of flights.
Algorithm:
  1. Check if f is null. If null, return.
  2. Find f in the flights list. If found, remove it.
  3. If not found, do nothing.
Related Features: flights (List<Flight>)
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f : Flight — the flight object to be removed.
Output: void
Pre-condition: f is not null.
Post-condition: The flights list no longer contains f.

Airline::publishFlight
Summary: Publishes a flight to make it available for booking, after validating timestamps, temporal consistency, and route integrity.
Algorithm:
  1. Check if f is null. If null, return false.
  2. Check if f.openForBooking is false. If true, return false (already published).
  3. Validate that f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are in "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss" format. If invalid, return false.
  4. Check: now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime. If not, return false.
  5. Check: f.departureAirport != f.arrivalAirport. If equal, return false.
  6. Set f.openForBooking = true.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: flights list, Flight.openForBooking, Flight.departureTime, Flight.arrivalTime, Flight.departureAirport, Flight.arrivalAirport
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
Input: f : Flight — the flight to publish.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp.
Output: boolean — true if the flight was successfully published; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: f is not null and is not already published (openForBooking == false).
Post-condition: If true, f.openForBooking is set to true.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.

# Operation Specifications
Booking::createReservation
Summary: Creates a single reservation for a given passenger on a given flight, assigning a unique ID and setting status to PENDING.
Algorithm:
  1. Generate a unique reservation ID (e.g., UUID or incremental counter).
  2. Create a new Passenger object with the given passenger name.
  3. Create a new Reservation object with:
       - id = generated unique ID
       - status = PENDING
       - flight = f
       - passenger = passenger
  4. Add the new reservation to this Booking's reservations list.
  5. Add the new reservation to the flight's reservations list.
  6. Return true.
Related Features: reservations list, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Reservation.flight, Reservation.passenger
Related Classes: Reservation, Passenger, Flight, ReservationStatus
Input: f : Flight — the flight for which the reservation is made.
       passenger : String — the name of the passenger.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp (used for validation in calling method).
Output: boolean — always returns true if pre-conditions are met (failure handled by caller).
Pre-condition: f is not null. passenger name is not null/empty. The caller ensures no duplicate passenger on the flight and current time before departure.
Post-condition: A new Reservation with status PENDING is added to both this Booking's reservations and the Flight's reservations.

       - passenger = passenger
  4. Add the new reservation to this Booking's reservations list.
  5. Add the new reservation to the flight's reservations list.
  6. Return true.
Related Features: reservations list, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Reservation.flight, Reservation.passenger
Related Classes: Reservation, Passenger, Flight, ReservationStatus
Input: f : Flight — the flight for which the reservation is made.
       passenger : String — the name of the passenger.
       now : EDate — the current timestamp (used for validation in calling method).
Output: boolean — true if the reservation was created successfully; false if the flight has already departed or the passenger already has a reservation on this flight.
Pre-condition: f is not null. passenger name is not null/empty.
Post-condition: If successful, a new Reservation with status PENDING is added to both this Booking's reservations and the Flight's reservations. Returns false if the flight has already departed or the passenger already has a reservation on this flight.}

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

## EMF Coding Style

```java
  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * 
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean addStopover(final Stopover stop, final Date now)
  {
    if (stop == null) return false;
    if (now.after(stop.getArrivalTime()) || now.equals(stop.getArrivalTime())) return false;
    getStopovers().add(stop);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Removes a stopover from the flight, ensuring the current time is before the stopover's arrival time.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If stop is null, return false.
   *   2. Check if now < stop.arrivalTime. If not, return false (stopover already in progress or past).
   *   3. Find stop in this.stopovers list. If not found, return false.
   *   4. Remove stop from this.stopovers list.
   *   5. Return true.
   * Related Features: stopovers list, Stopover.arrivalTime
   * Related Classes: Stopover, EDate
   * Input: stop : Stopover — the stopover to remove.
   *        now : EDate — the current timestamp.
   * Output: boolean — true if the stopover was removed successfully; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: stop is not null.
   * Post-condition: If true, stop is removed from the flight's stopovers list.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean removeStopover(final Stopover stop, final Date now)
  {
    if (stop == null) return false;
    if (!now.before(stop.getArrivalTime())) return false;
    if (!getStopovers().contains(stop)) return false;
    getStopovers().remove(stop);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Retrieves all confirmed reservations for this flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If this.openForBooking is false, return an empty list.
   *   2. Create an empty result list: List<Reservation> result.
   *   3. For each Reservation r in this.reservations:
   *        If r.status == CONFIRMED, add r to result.
   *   4. Return result.
   * Related Features: openForBooking, reservations list, Reservation.status
   * Related Classes: Reservation, ReservationStatus
   * Input: None
   * Output: List<Reservation> — list of confirmed reservations; empty list if flight is closed or none exist.
   * Pre-condition: None
   * Post-condition: Returns a list (possibly empty) of confirmed reservations.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model kind="operation"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<Reservation> getConfirmedReservations()
  {
    if (!isOpenForBooking()) return new BasicEList<Reservation>();
    EList<Reservation> result = new BasicEList<Reservation>();
    for (Reservation r : getReservations()) {
      if (r.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED) {
        result.add(r);
      }
    }
    return result;

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Removes a stopover from the flight, ensuring the current time is before the stopover's arrival time.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If stop is null, return false.
   *   2. Check if now < stop.arrivalTime. If not, return false (stopover already in progress or past).
   *   3. Find stop in this.stopovers list. If not found, return false.
   *   4. Remove stop from this.stopovers list.
   *   5. Return true.
   * Related Features: stopovers list, Stopover.arrivalTime
   * Related Classes: Stopover, EDate
   * Input: stop : Stopover — the stopover to remove.
   *        now : EDate — the current timestamp.
   * Output: boolean — true if the stopover was removed successfully; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: stop is not null.
   * Post-condition: If true, stop is removed from the flight's stopovers list.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean removeStopover(final Stopover stop, final Date now)
  {
    if (stop == null) return false;
    if (!now.before(stop.getArrivalTime())) return false;
    if (!getStopovers().contains(stop)) return false;
    getStopovers().remove(stop);
    return true;
  }

    for (Reservation r : getReservations()) {

      }
    }

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
public boolean createReservation(final Flight f, final String passenger, final Date now)
{
    // Check if flight has already departed
    if (f.getDepartureTime() != null && f.getDepartureTime().before(now)) {
      return false;
    }
    
    // Check if the passenger already has a reservation on this flight
    for (Reservation existingReservation : f.getReservations()) {
      if (existingReservation.getPassenger() != null && 
          passenger.equals(existingReservation.getPassenger().getName())) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    
    // 1. Generate a unique reservation ID
    String uniqueId = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    
    // 2. Create a new Passenger object
    Passenger newPassenger = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createPassenger();
    newPassenger.setName(passenger);
    
    // 3. Create a new Reservation object
    Reservation reservation = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createReservation();
    reservation.setId(uniqueId);
    reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
    reservation.setFlight(f);
    reservation.setPassenger(newPassenger);
    
    // 4. Add to this Booking's reservations list
    this.getReservations().add(reservation);
    
    // 5. Add to the flight's reservations list
    f.getReservations().add(reservation);
    
    // 6. Return true
    return true;
}
```

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Creates a single reservation for a given passenger on a given flight, assigning a unique ID and setting status to PENDING.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Generate a unique reservation ID (e.g., UUID or incremental counter).
   *   2. Create a new Passenger object with the given passenger name.
   *   3. Create a new Reservation object with:
   *        - id = generated unique ID
   *        - status = PENDING
   *        - flight = f
   *        - passenger = passenger
   *   4. Add the new reservation to this Booking's reservations list.
   *   5. Add the new reservation to the flight's reservations list.
   *   6. Return true.
   * Related Features: reservations list, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Reservation.flight, Reservation.passenger
   * Related Classes: Reservation, Passenger, Flight, ReservationStatus
   * Input: f : Flight — the flight for which the reservation is made.
   *        passenger : String — the name of the passenger.
   *        now : EDate — the current timestamp (used for validation in calling method).
   * Output: boolean — true if the reservation was created successfully; false if the flight has already departed or the passenger already has a reservation on this flight.
   * Pre-condition: f is not null. passenger name is not null/empty.
   * Post-condition: If successful, a new Reservation with status PENDING is added to both this Booking's reservations and the Flight's reservations. Returns false if the flight has already departed or the passenger already has a reservation on this flight.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean createReservation(final Flight f, final String passenger, final Date now)
  {
    // Check if the flight has already departed
    if (f.getDepartureTime() != null && f.getDepartureTime().before(now)) {
      return false;
    }
    
    // Check if the passenger already has a reservation on this flight
    for (Reservation existingReservation : f.getReservations()) {
      if (existingReservation.getPassenger() != null && 
          passenger.equals(existingReservation.getPassenger().getName())) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    
    // 1. Generate a unique reservation ID
    String uniqueId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    
    // 2. Create a new Passenger object with the given passenger name
    Passenger newPassenger = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createPassenger();
    newPassenger.setName(passenger);
    
    // 3. Create a new Reservation object
    Reservation reservation = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createReservation();
    reservation.setId(uniqueId);
    reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
    reservation.setFlight(f);
    reservation.setPassenger(newPassenger);
    
    // 4. Add the new reservation to this Booking's reservations list
    this.getReservations().add(reservation);
    
    // 5. Add the new reservation to the flight's reservations list
    f.getReservations().add(reservation);
    
    // 6. Return true
    return true;
  }

   * Summary: Creates a new booking for this customer on a given flight for a list of passengers, after validating no duplicate passengers on the flight and that the flight has not departed.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Check if f is null or f.openForBooking is false. If so, return false.
   *   2. Check if now >= f.departureTime. If true, return false (flight already departed or departing now).
   *   3. For each passenger name in listOfPassengerNames:
   *        a. Check if any existing Reservation in f.reservations has a passenger with the same name. If found, return false (duplicate).
   *   4. Create a new Booking object:
   *        - customer = this
   *        - reservations = new empty list
   *   5. For each passenger name in listOfPassengerNames:
   *        a. Call this new Booking's createReservation(f, passengerName, now).
   *   6. Add this new Booking to this customer's bookings list.
   *   7. Return true.
   * Related Features: bookings list, Flight.openForBooking, Flight.departureTime, Flight.reservations, Reservation.passenger.name
   * Related Classes: Flight, Booking, Reservation, Passenger, EDate
   * Input: f : Flight — the flight to book.
   *        now : EDate — the current timestamp.
   *        listOfPassengerNames : List<String> — list of passenger names (non-null, non-empty).
   * Output: boolean — true if booking was successful; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: f is not null, listOfPassengerNames is not null and contains at least one name.
   * Post-condition: If true, a new Booking is added to this customer, containing a PENDING Reservation for each passenger, and those reservations are also added to the flight.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information

  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.

  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;

  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.

  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
/**
 * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
 * <!-- end-user-doc -->
 * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
 * Summary: Creates a new booking for this customer on a given flight for a list of passengers, after validating no duplicate passengers on the flight and that the flight has not departed.
 * ...
 * <!-- end-model-doc -->
 * @model listOfPassengerNamesMany="true"
 *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
 * @generated NOT
 */
public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
{
    // 1. Check if f is null or f.openForBooking is false
    if (f == null || !f.isOpenForBooking()) {
        return false;
    }
    // 2. Check if now >= f.departureTime
    if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
        return false;
    }
    // 3. For each passenger name, check for duplicates
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
        for (Reservation res : f.getReservations()) {
            if (res.getPassenger().getName().equals(passengerName)) {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    // 4. Create a new Booking
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    // 5. For each passenger name, create a reservation
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
        booking.createReservation(f, passengerName, now);
    }
    // 6. Add booking to this customer's bookings list
    this.getBookings().add(booking);
    // 7. Return true
    return true;
}
```

For confirm and cancel, they are very similar:

```java
public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
{
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
        for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
            if (r.getId().equals(reservationID)) {
                Flight f = r.getFlight();
                if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
                    return false;
                }
                if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
                    return false;
                }
                r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}
```

```java
public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)
{
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
        for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
            if (r.getId().equals(reservationID)) {
                Flight f = r.getFlight();
                if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
                    return false;
                }
                if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
                    return false;
                }
                r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Creates a new booking for this customer on a given flight for a list of passengers, after validating no duplicate passengers on the flight and that the flight has not departed.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Check if f is null or f.openForBooking is false. If so, return false.
   *   2. Check if now >= f.departureTime. If true, return false (flight already departed or departing now).
   *   3. For each passenger name in listOfPassengerNames:
   *        a. Check if any existing Reservation in f.reservations has a passenger with the same name. If found, return false (duplicate).
   *   4. Create a new Booking object:
   *        - customer = this
   *        - reservations = new empty list
   *   5. For each passenger name in listOfPassengerNames:
   *        a. Call this new Booking's createReservation(f, passengerName, now).
   *   6. Add this new Booking to this customer's bookings list.
   *   7. Return true.
   * Related Features: bookings list, Flight.openForBooking, Flight.departureTime, Flight.reservations, Reservation.passenger.name
   * Related Classes: Flight, Booking, Reservation, Passenger, EDate
   * Input: f : Flight — the flight to book.
   *        now : EDate — the current timestamp.
   *        listOfPassengerNames : List<String> — list of passenger names (non-null, non-empty).
   * Output: boolean — true if booking was successful; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: f is not null, listOfPassengerNames is not null and contains at least one name.
   * Post-condition: If true, a new Booking is added to this customer, containing a PENDING Reservation for each passenger, and those reservations are also added to the flight.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->

  public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
  {
    // 1. Check if f is null or f.openForBooking is false
    if (f == null || !f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }
    // 2. Check if now >= f.departureTime
    if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
      return false;
    }
    // 3. For each passenger name, check for duplicates
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
      for (Reservation res : f.getReservations()) {
        if (res.getPassenger().getName().equals(passengerName)) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }
    // 4. Create a new Booking object
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    // 5. For each passenger name, create a reservation
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
      booking.createReservation(f, passengerName, now);
    }
    // 6. Add this new Booking to this customer's bookings list
    this.getBookings().add(booking);
    // 7. Return true
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Confirms an existing reservation belonging to this customer, provided the flight has not departed and is still open for booking.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Search through all bookings of this customer. For each Booking b:
   *        For each Reservation r in b.reservations:
   *            If r.id equals reservationID:
   *                a. Let f = r.flight.
   *                b. Check if now >= f.departureTime. If true, return false (flight already departed).
   *                c. Check if f.openForBooking is false. If false, return false (flight closed).
   *                d. Set r.status = CONFIRMED.
   *                e. Return true.
   *   2. If reservationID not found in any of this customer's bookings, return false.
   * Related Features: bookings list, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Reservation.flight, Flight.departureTime, Flight.openForBooking
   * Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, EDate, ReservationStatus
   * Input: reservationID : String — the unique ID of the reservation to confirm.
   *        now : EDate — the current timestamp.
   * Output: boolean — true if confirmation was successful; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: reservationID is not null.
   * Post-condition: If true, the reservation's status is CONFIRMED.

   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // 1. Search through all bookings of this customer
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (r.getId().equals(reservationID)) {
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          // b. Check if now >= f.departureTime
          if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
            return false;
          }
          // c. Check if f.openForBooking is false
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          // d. Set r.status = CONFIRMED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          // e. Return true
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    // 2. If reservationID not found
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Cancels an existing reservation belonging to this customer, provided the flight has not departed and is still open for booking.

  public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

```java
/**
 * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
 * <!-- end-user-doc -->
 * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
 * Summary: Creates a new booking for this customer on a given flight for a list of passengers, after validating no duplicate passengers on the flight and that the flight has not departed.
 * ...
 * <!-- end-model-doc -->
 * @model listOfPassengerNamesMany="true"
 *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
 * @generated NOT
 */
public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
{
    // 1. Check if f is null or f.openForBooking is false
    if (f == null || !f.isOpenForBooking()) {
        return false;
    }
    // 2. Check if now >= f.departureTime
    if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
        return false;
    }
    // 3. For each passenger name, check for duplicates
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
        for (Reservation res : f.getReservations()) {
            if (res.getPassenger().getName().equals(passengerName)) {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    // 4. Create a new Booking
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    // 5. For each passenger name, create a reservation
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
        booking.createReservation(f, passengerName, now);
    }
    // 6. Add booking to this customer's bookings list
    this.getBookings().add(booking);
    // 7. Return true
    return true;
}
```

```java
public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
{
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
        for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
            if (r.getId().equals(reservationID)) {
                Flight f = r.getFlight();
                if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
                    return false;
                }
                if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
                    return false;
                }
                r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

   * Summary: Creates a new booking for this customer on a given flight for a list of passengers, after validating no duplicate passengers on the flight and that the flight has not departed.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Check if f is null or f.openForBooking is false. If so, return false.
   *   2. Check if now >= f.departureTime. If true, return false (flight already departed or departing now).
   *   3. For each passenger name in listOfPassengerNames:
   *        a. Check if any existing Reservation in f.reservations has a passenger with the same name. If found, return false (duplicate).
   *   4. Create a new Booking object:
   *        - customer = this
   *        - reservations = new empty list
   *   5. For each passenger name in listOfPassengerNames:
   *        a. Call this new Booking's createReservation(f, passengerName, now).
   *   6. Add this new Booking to this customer's bookings list.
   *   7. Return true.

   * Pre-condition: f is not null, listOfPassengerNames is not null and contains at least one name.
   * Post-condition: If true, a new Booking is added to this customer, containing a PENDING Reservation for each passenger, and those reservations are also added to the flight.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->

      return false;
    }
    // 2. Check if now >= f.departureTime
    if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
      return false;
    }
    // 3. For each passenger name, check for duplicates

      for (Reservation res : f.getReservations()) {
        if (res.getPassenger().getName().equals(passengerName)) {
          return false;
        }

    }
    // 6. Add this new Booking to this customer's bookings list
    this.getBookings().add(booking);

    return true;
  }
