  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // 1. Search through all bookings of this customer
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (r.getId().equals(reservationID)) {
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          // b. Check if now >= f.departureTime
          if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
            return false;
          }
          // c. Check if f.openForBooking is false
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          // d. Set r.status = CONFIRMED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          // e. Return true
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    // 2. If reservationID not found
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Creates a new booking for this customer on a given flight for a list of passengers, after validating no duplicate passengers on the flight and that the flight has not departed.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Check if f is null or f.openForBooking is false. If so, return false.
   *   2. Check if now >= f.departureTime. If true, return false (flight already departed or departing now).
   *   3. For each passenger name in listOfPassengerNames:
   *        a. Check if any existing Reservation in f.reservations has a passenger with the same name. If found, return false (duplicate).
   *   4. Create a new Booking object:
   *        - customer = this
   *        - reservations = new empty list
   *   5. For each passenger name in listOfPassengerNames:
   *        a. Call this new Booking's createReservation(f, passengerName, now).
   *   6. Add this new Booking to this customer's bookings list.
   *   7. Return true.
   * Related Features: bookings list, Flight.openForBooking, Flight.departureTime, Flight.reservations, Reservation.passenger.name
   * Related Classes: Flight, Booking, Reservation, Passenger, EDate
   * Input: f : Flight — the flight to book.
   *        now : EDate — the current timestamp.
   *        listOfPassengerNames : List<String> — list of passenger names (non-null, non-empty).
   * Output: boolean — true if booking was successful; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: f is not null, listOfPassengerNames is not null and contains at least one name.
   * Post-condition: If true, a new Booking is added to this customer, containing a PENDING Reservation for each passenger, and those reservations are also added to the flight.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model listOfPassengerNamesMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
  {
    // 1. Check if f is null or f.openForBooking is false
    if (f == null || !f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }
    // 2. Check if now >= f.departureTime
    if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
      return false;
    }
    // 3. For each passenger name, check for duplicates
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
      for (Reservation res : f.getReservations()) {
        if (res.getPassenger().getName().equals(passengerName)) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }
    // 4. Create a new Booking object
    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);
    // 5. For each passenger name, create a reservation
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
      booking.createReservation(f, passengerName, now);
    }
    // 6. Add this new Booking to this customer's bookings list
    this.getBookings().add(booking);
    // 7. Return true
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Confirms an existing reservation belonging to this customer, provided the flight has not departed and is still open for booking.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Search through all bookings of this customer. For each Booking b:
   *        For each Reservation r in b.reservations:
   *            If r.id equals reservationID:
   *                a. Let f = r.flight.
   *                b. Check if now >= f.departureTime. If true, return false (flight already departed).
   *                c. Check if f.openForBooking is false. If false, return false (flight closed).
   *                d. Set r.status = CONFIRMED.
   *                e. Return true.
   *   2. If reservationID not found in any of this customer's bookings, return false.
   * Related Features: bookings list, Reservation.id, Reservation.status, Reservation.flight, Flight.departureTime, Flight.openForBooking
   * Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, EDate, ReservationStatus
   * Input: reservationID : String — the unique ID of the reservation to confirm.
   *        now : EDate — the current timestamp.
   * Output: boolean — true if confirmation was successful; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: reservationID is not null.
   * Post-condition: If true, the reservation's status is CONFIRMED.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // 1. Search through all bookings of this customer
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (r.getId().equals(reservationID)) {
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          // b. Check if now >= f.departureTime
          if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
            return false;
          }
          // c. Check if f.openForBooking is false
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          // d. Set r.status = CONFIRMED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          // e. Return true
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    // 2. If reservationID not found
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->

   * Post-condition: If true, the reservation's status is CANCELED.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    // 1. Search through all bookings of this customer
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations()) {
        if (r.getId().equals(reservationID)) {
          Flight f = r.getFlight();
          // b. Check if now >= f.departureTime
          if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
            return false;
          }
          // c. Check if f.openForBooking is false
          if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          // d. Set r.status = CANCELED
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
          // e. Return true
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    // 2. If reservationID not found
    return false;
  }

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information

  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.

  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  public boolean closeFlight(final String flightId, final Date now)
  {
    Flight flight = null;
    for (Flight f : getFlights()) {
      if (f.getId() != null && f.getId().equals(flightId)) {
        flight = f;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (flight == null) return false;
    if (!flight.isOpenForBooking()) return false;
    if (!flight.getDepartureTime().after(now)) return false;
    
    SimpleDateFormat dateFormat = new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd");
    String nowDate = dateFormat.format(now);
    String departureDate = dateFormat.format(flight.getDepartureTime());
    if (nowDate.equals(departureDate)) return false;
    
    flight.setOpenForBooking(false);
    
    for (Reservation r : flight.getReservations()) {
      if (r.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED) {
        r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

    }
    return result;
  }
